                "parent_id": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Optional parent goal ID or short ID if this is a subtask.",
                },
                "description": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "default": "",
                    "description": "Detailed context, acceptance criteria, or instructions.",
                },
                "priority": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": ["low", "medium", "high", "critical"],
                    "default": "medium",
                    "description": "Priority tier of the work item.",
                },
                "tags": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": {"type": "string"},
                    "description": "Optional categorisation tags.",
                },
                "namespace": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Optional namespace override.",
                },
            },
            "required": ["title"],
        },
    ),
    types.Tool(
        name="mesh_task_update",
        description="Update status, priority, or notes of an existing task or goal.",
        inputSchema={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "item_id": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "UUID or short ID (e.g. 'task-1', 'goal-2') of the work item.",
                },
                "status": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": ["todo", "in_progress", "blocked", "done", "cancelled", "reopened"],
                    "description": "New lifecycle status (use 'reopened' to reactivate a done or cancelled item).",
                },
                "priority": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": ["low", "medium", "high", "critical"],
                    "description": "Updated priority tier.",
                },
                "title": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Updated title.",
                },
                "description": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Updated description.",
                },
                "note": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Reason for status transition or blocker notes.",
                },
                "namespace": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Optional namespace override.",
                },
            },
            "required": ["item_id"],
        },
    ),
    types.Tool(
        name="mesh_task_complete",
        description="Mark a task or goal completed with a mandatory summary of changes and optional evidence.",
        inputSchema={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "item_id": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "UUID or short ID of the completed work item.",
                },
                "summary": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Detailed summary of what was completed, decisions made, or changes made.",
                },
                "evidence": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": {"type": "string"},
                    "description": "Optional list of modified file paths, git commit SHAs, or links.",
                },
                "namespace": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Optional namespace override.",
                },
            },
            "required": ["item_id", "summary"],
        },
    ),
    types.Tool(
        name="mesh_task_list",
        description="List tasks and goals in the namespace with deterministic priority and creation-date sorting.",
        inputSchema={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "status": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": ["all", "open", "closed", "todo", "in_progress", "blocked", "done", "cancelled"],
                    "default": "open",
                    "description": "Filter by status: 'open' (todo/in_progress/blocked), 'closed' (done/cancelled), or exact status.",
                },
                "kind": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": ["task", "goal"],
                    "description": "Filter by kind ('task' or 'goal').",
                },
                "parent_id": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Filter subtasks of a specific goal.",
                },
                "limit": {
                    "type": "integer",
                    "default": 20,
                    "description": "Maximum number of items to return.",
                },
                "namespace": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Optional namespace override.",
                },
            },
        },
    ),
    types.Tool(
        name="mesh_log",
        description="Generate a project changelog combining completed tasks, evidence, and architectural decisions over a time window.",
        inputSchema={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "since": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "default": "7d",
                    "description": "Time window (e.g. '24h', '7d', '30d') or ISO timestamp.",
                },
                "format": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": ["markdown", "json"],
                    "default": "markdown",
                    "description": "Output format ('markdown' or 'json').",
                },
                "namespace": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Optional namespace override.",
                },
            },
        },
    ),
]


@app.list_tools()
async def list_tools() -> list[types.Tool]:
    return _MCP_TOOLS


def _parse_arg_bool(
    arguments: dict[str, Any],
    key: str,
    default: Optional[bool] = False,
) -> Optional[bool]:
    if key not in arguments or arguments[key] is None:
        return default
    return parse_strict_bool(arguments[key], default=bool(default), raise_on_invalid=True)


def _execute_tool_sync_impl(
    name: str,
    arguments: dict[str, Any],
    default_namespace: Optional[str] = None,
    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,
    allow_daemon_proxy: bool = True,
    context: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Execute a tool synchronously with thread-safe stdout redirection for MCP stdio safety."""
    with _track_foreground_tool_call():
        ns_override = arguments.get("namespace") or default_namespace or _get_default_namespace()
        try:
            validated_ns = validate_namespace(ns_override)
        except ValueError as exc:
            return {"error": str(exc), "status": "error"}
        arguments = {**arguments, "namespace": validated_ns}

        if allow_daemon_proxy:
            proxied = _try_proxy_via_daemon(name, arguments)
            if proxied is not None:
                return proxied

        try:
            with _safe_redirect_stdout_to_stderr():
                if name == "mesh_learn":
                    content_raw = arguments["content"]
                    req_local_only = bool(_parse_arg_bool(arguments, "local_only", False))
                    req_vault = bool(_parse_arg_bool(arguments, "vault_secrets", False))
                    guard_res = guard_ingest(
                        content_raw,
                        local_only=req_local_only,
                    )
                    if guard_res.rejected:
                        return {
                            "status": "rejected",
                            "error": f"Memory rejected: {guard_res.reason}",
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                        }
                    try:
                        clean_tags, tags_priv, tag_warns = guard_tags(
                            arguments.get("tags", []),
                            local_only=guard_res.local_only,
                        )
                    except ValueError as tag_exc:
                        return {
                            "status": "rejected",
                            "error": f"Memory rejected: {tag_exc}",
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                        }

                    mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=True)
                    result = mesh.learn(
                        content=content_raw if req_vault else guard_res.content,
                        memory_type=arguments.get("memory_type", "fact"),
                        confidence=float(arguments.get("confidence", 1.0)),
                        tags=clean_tags,
                        ttl_days=arguments.get("ttl_days"),
                        local_only=bool(guard_res.local_only or tags_priv),
                        secret_ref=arguments.get("secret_ref"),
                        vault_secrets=req_vault,
                        trust_tier="agent",
                    )
                    eng = get_engine_status()
                    response: dict[str, Any] = {
                        "status": result["status"],
                        "memory_id": result["memory_id"],
                        "deduplicated": result.get("deduplicated", False),
                        "local_only": bool(
                            result.get("local_only", guard_res.local_only or tags_priv)
                        ),
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "total_memories": mesh.count(),
                        "trust_tier": result.get("trust_tier", "agent"),
                        "quarantined": bool(result.get("quarantined", False)),
                        "embedding_backend": result.get("embedding_backend", eng.get("backend", "unloaded")),
                        "embedding_degraded": bool(result.get("embedding_degraded", eng.get("embedding_degraded", False))),
                    }
                    if result.get("secret_ref"):
                        response["secret_ref"] = result["secret_ref"]
                    if result.get("namespace_created"):
                        response["namespace_created"] = True
                        response["similar_existing"] = result.get("similar_existing", [])
                    all_warnings = tuple([*guard_res.warnings, *tag_warns])
                    if all_warnings:
                        response["warnings"] = all_warnings
                    if result.get("conflict"):
                        response["conflict_warning"] = (
                            "A potentially contradictory memory already exists. "
                            "Review and delete or update the old one if this new memory supersedes it."
                        )
                        response["conflict"] = result["conflict"]
                    return response

                elif name == "mesh_recall":
                    limit_n = int(arguments.get("n") or arguments.get("count") or 5)
                    include_other = bool(_parse_arg_bool(arguments, "include_other_possible", True))
                    include_stale = bool(_parse_arg_bool(arguments, "include_stale", True))
                    include_quarantined = bool(_parse_arg_bool(arguments, "include_quarantined", False))
                    max_other = int(arguments.get("max_other_possible", 5)) if include_other else 0
                    inc_priv = _resolve_mcp_include_private(arguments, context)
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        eng = get_engine_status()
                        return {
                            "query": str(arguments.get("query", "")).strip(),
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                            "count": 0,
                            "total_in_namespace": 0,
                            "stale_count": 0,
                            "results": [],
                            "other_possible_memories": [],
                            "filter_hint": None,
                            "embedding_backend": eng.get("backend", "unloaded"),
                            "embedding_degraded": bool(eng.get("embedding_degraded", False)),
                            "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                            "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                        }

                    res = mesh.recall_with_context(
                        query=arguments["query"],
                        n=limit_n,
                        min_similarity=float(arguments.get("min_similarity", 0.0)),
                        min_confidence=float(arguments.get("min_confidence", 0.0)),
                        include_stale=include_stale,
                        memory_type=arguments.get("memory_type"),
                        tags=arguments.get("tags"),
                        source_agent=arguments.get("source_agent"),
                        max_other_possible=max_other,
                        include_private=inc_priv,
                        include_quarantined=include_quarantined,
                    )
                    res["namespace_exists"] = True
                    res["untrusted_reference_data"] = True
                    res["notice"] = _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE
                    return res

                elif name == "mesh_update":
                    update_local_only = _parse_arg_bool(arguments, "local_only", None)
                    req_clear_ttl = bool(_parse_arg_bool(arguments, "clear_ttl", False))
                    guard_res = None
                    if arguments.get("content") is not None:
                        guard_res = guard_ingest(
                            str(arguments.get("content")),
                            local_only=bool(update_local_only),
                        )
                        if guard_res.rejected:
                            return {
                                "status": "rejected",
                                "error": f"Memory rejected: {guard_res.reason}",
                                "namespace": validated_ns,
                            }
                    raw_tags = arguments.get("tags")
                    update_clean_tags: Optional[list[str]] = None
                    update_tag_warns: tuple[str, ...] = ()
                    tags_priv = False
                    if raw_tags is not None:
                        try:
                            update_clean_tags, tags_priv, update_tag_warns = guard_tags(
                                raw_tags,
                                local_only=bool(
                                    (guard_res.local_only if guard_res else False) or update_local_only
                                ),
                            )
                        except ValueError as tag_exc:
                            return {
                                "status": "rejected",
                                "error": f"Memory rejected: {tag_exc}",
                                "namespace": validated_ns,
                            }
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "status": "error",
                            "error": f"Namespace '{validated_ns}' does not exist.",
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                        }
                    updated = mesh.update(
                        memory_id=arguments["memory_id"],
                        content=guard_res.content if guard_res else arguments.get("content"),
                        memory_type=arguments.get("memory_type"),
                        confidence=arguments.get("confidence"),
                        tags=update_clean_tags,
                        ttl_days=arguments.get("ttl_days"),
                        clear_ttl=req_clear_ttl,
                        local_only=(
                            True
                            if ((guard_res is not None and guard_res.local_only) or tags_priv)
                            else update_local_only
                        ),
                        secret_ref=arguments.get("secret_ref"),
                    )
                    update_resp: dict[str, Any] = {
                        "status": "updated",
                        "memory_id": updated["memory_id"],
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "memory": updated["memory"],
                    }
                    all_warnings = tuple([*(guard_res.warnings if guard_res else ()), *update_tag_warns])
                    if all_warnings:
                        update_resp["warnings"] = all_warnings
                    return update_resp

                elif name == "mesh_approve":
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "status": "error",
                            "error": f"Namespace '{validated_ns}' does not exist.",
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                        }
                    approved = mesh.approve(arguments["memory_id"])
                    return {
                        "status": "approved",
                        "memory_id": approved["memory_id"],
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "memory": approved["memory"],
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_forget":
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "status": "not_found",
                            "memory_id": arguments["memory_id"],
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                            "remaining": 0,
                        }
                    deleted = mesh.forget(arguments["memory_id"])
                    return {
                        "status": "deleted" if deleted else "not_found",
                        "memory_id": arguments["memory_id"],
                        "remaining": mesh.count(),
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_inspect":
                    inc_priv = _resolve_mcp_include_private(arguments, context)
                    quarantined_only = bool(_parse_arg_bool(arguments, "quarantined_only", False))
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                            "total": 0,
                            "memories": [],
                            "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                            "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                        }
                    mem_type = arguments.get("memory_type")
                    tag_filter = arguments.get("tag")
                    tags_filter = arguments.get("tags")
                    memories = mesh.inspect(
                        limit=int(arguments.get("limit", 10)),
                        memory_type=mem_type,
                        tag=tag_filter,
                        tags=tags_filter,
                        include_private=inc_priv,
                        quarantined_only=quarantined_only,
                    )
                    total_matching = (
                        len(mesh.inspect(limit=10000, memory_type=mem_type, tag=tag_filter, tags=tags_filter, include_private=inc_priv, quarantined_only=True))
                        if quarantined_only
                        else mesh.count_matching(
                            memory_type=mem_type,
                            tag=tag_filter,
                            tags=tags_filter,
                            include_private=inc_priv,
                        )
                    )
                    return {
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "namespace_exists": True,
                        "total": total_matching,
                        "memories": memories,
                        "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                        "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_context":
                    inc_priv = _resolve_mcp_include_private(arguments, context)
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                            "format": arguments.get("format", "markdown"),
                            "context": "",
                        }
                    bundle = build_context_bundle(
                        query=arguments.get("query"),
                        namespace=mesh.namespace,
                        count=int(arguments.get("count") or 10),
                        max_memories=arguments.get("max_memories"),
                        n=arguments.get("n"),
                        format=arguments.get("format", "markdown"),
                        min_confidence=float(arguments.get("min_confidence", 0.3)),
                        memory_type=arguments.get("memory_type"),
                        tags=arguments.get("tags"),
                        include_private=inc_priv,
                        mesh_instance=mesh,
                    )
                    out_ctx: dict[str, Any] = {
                        "namespace": bundle["namespace"],
                        "namespace_exists": bundle.get("namespace_exists", True),
                        "format": bundle["format"],
                        "context": bundle["context"],
                    }
                    if "did_you_mean" in bundle:
                        out_ctx["did_you_mean"] = bundle["did_you_mean"]
                    return out_ctx

                elif name == "mesh_patterns":
                    inc_priv = _resolve_mcp_include_private(arguments, context)
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                            "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                            "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                            "top_topics": [],
                            "memory_gaps": [],
                            "unused_memories": [],
                            "stale_memories": [],
                        }
                    patterns = mesh.patterns(include_private=inc_priv)
                    return {
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "namespace_exists": True,
                        "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                        "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                        **patterns,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_contradictions":
                    resolved = bool(_parse_arg_bool(arguments, "resolved", False))
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                            "resolved": resolved,
                            "count": 0,
                            "contradictions": [],
                        }
                    conflicts = mesh.contradictions(resolved=resolved)
                    return {
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "namespace_exists": True,
                        "resolved": resolved,
                        "count": len(conflicts),
                        "contradictions": conflicts,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_resolve_contradiction":
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "status": "error",
                            "error": f"Namespace '{validated_ns}' does not exist.",
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                        }
                    mesh.resolve_contradiction(arguments["contradiction_id"])
                    return {
                        "status": "resolved",
                        "contradiction_id": arguments["contradiction_id"],
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_sync":
                    summary_text = (arguments.get("summary") or "").strip()
                    include_history = bool(
                        _parse_arg_bool(arguments, "include_history", False)
                        or _parse_arg_bool(arguments, "all", False)
                    )
                    quarantine_arg = _parse_arg_bool(arguments, "quarantine", None)
                    sync_mesh: Optional[Mesh] = None
                    if summary_text:
                        guard_res = guard_ingest(summary_text, local_only=False)
                        if guard_res.rejected:
                            return {
                                "status": "rejected",
                                "error": f"Memory rejected: {guard_res.reason}",
                                "namespace": validated_ns,
                            }
                        sync_mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=True)
                        sync_mesh.learn(
                            content=guard_res.content,
                            memory_type="result",
                            tags=["session-sync"],
                            local_only=guard_res.local_only,
                            trust_tier="agent",
                        )

                    allow_log_sync = _ctx_flag(
                        context,
                        "allow_log_sync",
                        _env_flag("MESH_ENABLE_AUTO_SYNC") or _env_flag("MESH_ALLOW_LOG_SYNC"),
                    )
                    sync_status = get_sync_status()
                    if not allow_log_sync:
                        if sync_mesh is None:
                            try:
                                sync_mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                                total_mems = sync_mesh.count()
                            except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                                total_mems = 0
                        else:
                            total_mems = sync_mesh.count()
                        return {
                            "status": "synchronized",
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "manual_summary": summary_text or "none",
                            "log_capture": "disabled",
                            "hint": "Set MESH_ALLOW_LOG_SYNC=1 to enable",
                            "passive_memories_captured": 0,
                            "captured_count": 0,
                            "stored_count": 0,
                            "deduplicated_count": 0,
                            "error": None,
                            "last_sync_error": sync_status["last_error"],
                            "last_sync_error_at": sync_status["last_error_at"],
                            "total_memories": total_mems,
                        }

                    from .passive import extract_memories_from_log

                    log_dirs = detect_agent_logs()
                    if log_dirs and not include_history:
                        _seed_log_watermarks(log_dirs, include_history=False)

                    log_text, pending_wm = (
                        _collect_incremental_logs_pending(
                            log_dirs, include_history=include_history
                        )
                        if log_dirs
                        else ("", {})
                    )
                    ws_root = _ctx_workspace_root(context)
                    log_text = _filter_log_text_to_workspace(log_text, ws_root)

                    captured_count = 0
                    stored_count = 0
                    deduplicated_count = 0
                    extraction_err: Optional[str] = None

                    if log_text:
                        if sync_mesh is None:
                            sync_mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=True)
                        allow_cloud = _ctx_flag(
                            context, "allow_cloud_sync", _env_flag("MESH_ALLOW_CLOUD_SYNC")
                        )
                        try:
                            capture_res = extract_memories_from_log(
                                log_text,
                                namespace=validated_ns,
                                agent_id=sync_mesh.agent_id,
                                force_local=not allow_cloud,
                                assume_yes=allow_cloud,
                                default_local_only=not allow_cloud,
                                resolve_input=False,
                                quarantine=quarantine_arg,
                            )
                            stored_count = int(capture_res.get("stored_count", 0))
                            deduplicated_count = int(capture_res.get("deduplicated_count", 0))
                            if "captured_count" in capture_res:
                                captured_count = int(capture_res["captured_count"])
                            else:
                                captured_count = stored_count + deduplicated_count
                            extraction_err = capture_res.get("error")
                            if extraction_err:
                                _record_sync_error(str(extraction_err))
                            else:
                                _commit_log_watermarks(pending_wm)
                        except Exception as exc:
                            extraction_err = str(exc)
                            logger.exception("Foreground mesh_sync log extraction failed: %s", exc)
                            _record_sync_error(extraction_err)
                    elif pending_wm:
                        _commit_log_watermarks(pending_wm)

                    if sync_mesh is None:
                        try:
                            sync_mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                            total_mems = sync_mesh.count()
                        except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                            total_mems = 0
                    else:
                        total_mems = sync_mesh.count()

                    sync_status = get_sync_status()
                    return {
                        "status": "synchronized",
                        "namespace": validated_ns,
                        "manual_summary": summary_text or "none",
                        "log_capture": "enabled",
                        "passive_memories_captured": captured_count,
                        "captured_count": captured_count,
                        "stored_count": stored_count,
                        "deduplicated_count": deduplicated_count,
                        "error": extraction_err,
                        "last_sync_error": sync_status["last_error"],
                        "last_sync_error_at": sync_status["last_error_at"],
                        "total_memories": total_mems,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_project_brief":
                    inc_priv = _resolve_mcp_include_private(arguments, context)
                    max_tokens = int(arguments.get("max_tokens") or 500)
                    include_work = bool(_parse_arg_bool(arguments, "include_work_items", True))
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "namespace_state": "new",
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                            "total_memories": 0,
                            "open_tasks_count": 0,
                            "completed_recent_count": 0,
                            "pending_contradictions": 0,
                            "brief_markdown": (
                                f"# Project Brief: {validated_ns}\n\n"
                                f"*Namespace state:* `new` (empty)\n"
                                f"No memories, goals, or tasks exist yet in this namespace. "
                                f"Use `mesh_learn` to record durable conventions/facts and `mesh_task_create` to track work."
                            ),
                            "estimated_tokens": 30,
                            "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                            "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                        }
                    brief = mesh.project_brief(
                        max_tokens=max_tokens,
                        include_work_items=include_work,
                        include_private=inc_priv,
                    )
                    return {
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "namespace_exists": True,
                        "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                        "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                        **brief,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_task_create":
                    inc_priv = _resolve_mcp_include_private(arguments, context)
                    mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=True)
                    created = mesh.create_work_item(
                        title=str(arguments.get("title") or ""),
                        kind=str(arguments.get("kind") or "task"),
                        parent_id=arguments.get("parent_id"),
                        description=str(arguments.get("description") or ""),
                        priority=str(arguments.get("priority") or "medium"),
                        tags=arguments.get("tags"),
                        local_only=inc_priv,
                    )
                    return {
                        "status": "created",
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "id": created["id"],
                        "short_id": created["short_id"],
                        "item": created,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_task_update":
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "status": "error",
                            "error": f"Namespace '{validated_ns}' does not exist.",
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                        }
                    updated = mesh.update_work_item(
                        item_id=str(arguments.get("item_id") or ""),
                        status=arguments.get("status"),
                        priority=arguments.get("priority"),
                        title=arguments.get("title"),
                        description=arguments.get("description"),
                        note=arguments.get("note"),
                    )
                    return {
                        "status": "updated",
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "id": updated["id"],
                        "short_id": updated["short_id"],
                        "item": updated,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_task_complete":
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "status": "error",
                            "error": f"Namespace '{validated_ns}' does not exist.",
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                        }
                    summary = str(arguments.get("summary") or "").strip()
                    if not summary:
                        return {
                            "status": "error",
                            "error": "A non-empty summary of what was completed is required.",
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                        }
                    evidence = arguments.get("evidence")
                    completed = mesh.complete_work_item(
                        item_id=str(arguments.get("item_id") or ""),
                        summary=summary,
                        evidence=evidence if isinstance(evidence, list) else None,
                    )
                    return {
                        "status": "completed",
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "id": completed["id"],
                        "short_id": completed["short_id"],
                        "item": completed,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_task_list":
                    inc_priv = _resolve_mcp_include_private(arguments, context)
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                            "count": 0,
                            "items": [],
                            "work_items": [],
                            "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                            "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                        }
                    status_arg = arguments.get("status") or "open"
                    if status_arg == "all":
                        status_arg = None
                    items = mesh.list_work_items(
                        status=status_arg,
                        kind=arguments.get("kind"),
                        parent_id=arguments.get("parent_id"),
                        include_private=inc_priv,
                        limit=int(arguments.get("limit") or 20),
                    )
                    return {
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "namespace_exists": True,
                        "count": len(items),
                        "items": items,
                        "work_items": items,
                        "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                        "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                    }

                elif name == "mesh_log":
                    inc_priv = _resolve_mcp_include_private(arguments, context)
                    try:
                        mesh = get_mesh(namespace=validated_ns, agent_id=agent_id, create=False)
                    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
                        return {
                            "namespace": validated_ns,
                            "namespace_exists": False,
                            "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(validated_ns),
                            "since": str(arguments.get("since") or "7d"),
                            "changelog": "# Changelog\n\nNamespace does not exist yet.",
                        }
                    log_res = mesh.changelog(
                        since=str(arguments.get("since") or "7d"),
                        format=str(arguments.get("format") or "markdown"),
                        include_private=inc_priv,
                    )
                    return {
                        "namespace": mesh.namespace,
                        "namespace_exists": True,
                        "untrusted_reference_data": True,
                        "notice": _UNTRUSTED_NOTICE,
                        **log_res,
                    }

                else:
                    return {"error": f"Unknown tool: {name}", "status": "error"}
        except ValueError as exc:
            return {"error": str(exc), "status": "error"}


def _execute_tool_sync(
    name: str,
    arguments: dict[str, Any],
    default_namespace: Optional[str] = None,
    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,
    allow_daemon_proxy: bool = True,
    context: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Run a tool, deriving ownership enforcement from server configuration (`MESH_ENFORCE_AGENT_OWNERSHIP`).
    A caller-supplied `context` can never weaken server ownership enforcement (Issue H4).
    """
    server_enforced = _env_flag("MESH_ENFORCE_AGENT_OWNERSHIP")
    ctx_enforced = _ctx_flag(context, "enforce_agent_ownership", False) if context else False
    token = _OWNERSHIP_ENFORCEMENT_OVERRIDE.set(bool(server_enforced or ctx_enforced))
    try:
        return _execute_tool_sync_impl(
            name=name,
            arguments=arguments,
            default_namespace=default_namespace,
            agent_id=agent_id,
            allow_daemon_proxy=allow_daemon_proxy,
            context=context,
        )
    finally:
        if token is not None:
            _OWNERSHIP_ENFORCEMENT_OVERRIDE.reset(token)


class MeshCallToolResult(types.CallToolResult):
    """Subclass of types.CallToolResult that supports indexing and iteration for backwards compatibility."""

    def __getitem__(self, item: int) -> Any:
        return self.content[item]

    def __iter__(self) -> Any:  # pyright: ignore[reportIncompatibleMethodOverride]
        return iter(self.content)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.content)


@app.call_tool()
async def call_tool(name: str, arguments: Optional[dict[str, Any]]) -> MeshCallToolResult:
    args = arguments or {}
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    status_code = "ok"
    try:
        payload = _execute_tool_sync(name, args)
        is_error = False
        if isinstance(payload, dict):
            if payload.get("status") in ("rejected", "error") or (
                payload.get("error") is not None and payload.get("status") != "synchronized"
            ):
                is_error = True
                status_code = str(payload.get("status") or "error")
        raw_text = json.dumps(payload, indent=2)
        dur_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
        ns_used = str(args.get("namespace") or _get_default_namespace())
        log_audit_event(
            surface="mcp",
            tool_or_command=name,
            namespace=ns_used,
            duration_ms=dur_ms,
            status_code=status_code,
            agent_id=str(_get_default_agent_id()),
            payload_size_bytes=len(raw_text.encode("utf-8")),
            result_count=len(payload.get("items") or payload.get("memories") or []) if isinstance(payload, dict) else 0,
        )
        return MeshCallToolResult(
            isError=is_error,
            content=[types.TextContent(type="text", text=raw_text)],
        )
    except Exception as e:
        dur_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
        ns_used = str(args.get("namespace") or _get_default_namespace())
        log_audit_event(
            surface="mcp",
            tool_or_command=name,
            namespace=ns_used,
            duration_ms=dur_ms,
            status_code="error",
            agent_id=str(_get_default_agent_id()),
            payload_size_bytes=0,
            result_count=0,
        )
        return MeshCallToolResult(
            isError=True,
            content=[
                types.TextContent(
                    type="text",
                    text=json.dumps({"error": str(e), "tool": name, "status": "error"}, indent=2),
                )
            ],
        )


async def _run() -> None:
    global _STDIO_SERVER_ACTIVE
    _STDIO_SERVER_ACTIVE = True
    try:
        async with stdio_server() as (read_stream, write_stream):
            await app.run(
                read_stream,
                write_stream,
                app.create_initialization_options(),
            )
    finally:
        _STDIO_SERVER_ACTIVE = False


def run() -> None:
    """Entry point for: mesh-server"""
    no_daemon = _env_flag("MESH_NO_AUTO_DAEMON")
    auto_sync = _env_flag("MESH_ENABLE_AUTO_SYNC")
    auto_approve = _env_flag("MESH_AUTO_APPROVE")
    is_interactive = hasattr(sys.stdin, "isatty") and sys.stdin.isatty()

    if not no_daemon:
        if auto_sync or auto_approve:
            threading.Thread(target=background_sync_worker, daemon=True).start()
        elif is_interactive and request_user_permission("start the background MCP cache warmup worker"):
            threading.Thread(target=background_sync_worker, daemon=True).start()
    asyncio.run(_run())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

"""
mesh/memory.py — Core semantic & hybrid memory orchestration for Mesh.

Performance & Security Architecture:
  - Non-Blocking 384-dim `all-MiniLM-L6-v2` Embedding Engine:
      1. Direct `onnxruntime` + `tokenizers` (Zero-network local ONNX, ~80ms cold start, ~2ms inference)
      2. SHA-256 verified canonical ONNX model downloads with interactive `y/n` user permission
         and persistent status/error reporting in `~/.mesh/model_status.json`
      3. Deterministic 384-dim semantic fallback (<0.05ms) when offline, warming up, or declined
  - Read-Only `float32` NumPy `_EMBED_CACHE` (configurable via `MESH_EMBED_CACHE_SIZE`, default 1024),
    cleared on `unload_model()` so idle memory drops to ~0 MB.
  - Bounded `_PENDING_REEMBED_QUEUE` (capped at 10,000 `(namespace, memory_id)` entries, drop-oldest)
    and single-flight per-namespace background healing threads.
  - Security & Provenance Controls:
      - First-class `secret_ref` memory type and optional automatic keyring vaulting (Finding S1)
      - Per-agent access scopes (`MESH_AGENT_SCOPES` & `MESH_READ_ONLY`, Finding S6)
      - Provenance tiers (`trust_tier`: 'user' | 'agent' | 'log_captured'), quarantine filtering
        on `recall()`, and `Mesh.approve()` (Finding S8)
      - Full ingestion & update guard enforcement on content, tags, and visibility changes (Finding S11)
      - Recall query secret redaction and side-channel protection (Finding S12, N8)
      - Linear-time subject-value copula extraction preventing ReDoS inside write locks (Finding N3)
"""

# Bounded pending re-embed queue keyed by (namespace, memory_id), capped at 10,000 entries (Phase 3.2)
_PENDING_REEMBED_MAXSIZE = 10000
_PENDING_REEMBED_LOCK = threading.Lock()


class _BoundedReembedQueue:
    """
    Bounded queue of `(namespace, memory_id, content)` items awaiting ONNX re-embedding.
    Deduplicates by `(namespace, memory_id)` so the newest content wins and drops the
    oldest entry when capacity exceeds `maxsize` (default 10,000).
    """

    def __init__(self, maxsize: int = _PENDING_REEMBED_MAXSIZE) -> None:
        self.maxsize = max(1, int(maxsize))
        self._data: OrderedDict[tuple[str, str], str] = OrderedDict()

    def put(self, namespace: str, memory_id: str, content: str) -> None:
        key = (str(namespace), str(memory_id))
        self._data[key] = str(content)
        self._data.move_to_end(key)
        while len(self._data) > self.maxsize:
            self._data.popitem(last=False)

    def append(self, item: tuple[str, str, str]) -> None:
        ns, mid, content = item
        self.put(ns, mid, content)

    def pop_all(self) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
        items = [(ns, mid, text) for (ns, mid), text in self._data.items()]
        self._data.clear()
        return items

    def clear(self) -> None:
        self._data.clear()

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self._data)

    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[tuple[str, str, str]]:
        return iter([(ns, mid, text) for (ns, mid), text in self._data.items()])

    def __getitem__(self, index: Any) -> Any:
        as_list = [(ns, mid, text) for (ns, mid), text in self._data.items()]
        return as_list[index]

    def __bool__(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self._data)


_PENDING_REEMBED_QUEUE = _BoundedReembedQueue(_PENDING_REEMBED_MAXSIZE)

def _get_embed_cache_maxsize() -> int:
    raw = os.environ.get("MESH_EMBED_CACHE_SIZE", "").strip()
    if not raw:
        return 1024
    try:
        return max(0, int(raw))
    except ValueError:
        return 1024

    ("@c_limit", {"rate", "limit", "limits", "constraint", "constraints", "quota", "requests", "minute", "ip", "api"}),
    ("@c_lang", {"typescript", "javascript", "python", "rust", "go", "java", "language", "languages", "program", "programming", "code", "coding", "file", "files"}),
    ("@c_pet", {"cat", "cats", "dog", "dogs", "pet", "pets"}),
    ("@c_env", {"staging", "production", "prod", "dev", "environment", "server", "url", "port", "deploy", "script", "host"}),
    ("@c_mem", {"agent", "agents", "coordinate", "shared", "memory", "memories", "recall", "learn", "mesh"}),
]


# ==============================================================================
# Persistent Model Bootstrap Status (`~/.mesh/model_status.json`, Phase 3.3)
# ==============================================================================

_STATUS_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_LAST_MODEL_STATUS: dict[str, Any] = {
    "state": "uninitialized",
    "backend": "unloaded",
    "last_error": None,
    "last_attempt": None,
    "checksum_expected": None,
    "checksum_actual": None,
    "hint": None,
}


def _record_model_status(
    state: str,
    backend: Optional[str] = None,
    last_error: Optional[str] = None,
    checksum_expected: Optional[list[str]] = None,
    checksum_actual: Optional[str] = None,
    hint: Optional[str] = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Persist ONNX model bootstrap state and errors to `~/.mesh/model_status.json`."""
    now_iso = _utc_now_iso()
    with _STATUS_LOCK:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "state": state,
            "backend": backend or (_model_backend or "unloaded"),
            "last_error": last_error,
            "last_attempt": now_iso,
            "checksum_expected": checksum_expected,
            "checksum_actual": checksum_actual,
        }
        if hint:
            payload["hint"] = hint
        _LAST_MODEL_STATUS.update(payload)
        try:
            status_path = _get_model_status_path()
            ensure_secure_mesh_dir(status_path.parent)
            tmp_path = status_path.with_suffix(f".tmp.{os.getpid()}.{threading.get_ident()}")
            tmp_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            secure_file_permissions(tmp_path)
            os.replace(tmp_path, status_path)
            secure_file_permissions(status_path)
            try:
                st = status_path.stat()
                global _MODEL_STATUS_CACHE
                _MODEL_STATUS_CACHE = (status_path, int(st.st_mtime_ns), int(st.st_size), dict(payload))
            except OSError:
                pass
        except OSError:
            pass
        return dict(payload)


_MODEL_STATUS_CACHE: Optional[tuple[Path, int, int, dict[str, Any]]] = None


def get_model_status() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Read `~/.mesh/model_status.json` or return in-memory bootstrap status."""
    global _MODEL_STATUS_CACHE
    status_path = _get_model_status_path()
    try:
        st = status_path.stat()
        cache = _MODEL_STATUS_CACHE
        if (
            cache is not None
            and cache[0] == status_path
            and cache[1] == st.st_mtime_ns
            and cache[2] == st.st_size
        ):
            return dict(cache[3])
        data = json.loads(status_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if isinstance(data, dict):
            with _STATUS_LOCK:
                _MODEL_STATUS_CACHE = (status_path, int(st.st_mtime_ns), int(st.st_size), data)
            return dict(data)
    except Exception:
        pass
    with _STATUS_LOCK:
        return dict(_LAST_MODEL_STATUS)


def _make_readonly_f32(vec: Any) -> np.ndarray:
    arr = np.asarray(vec, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1).copy()
    arr.setflags(write=False)
    return arr


def _normalize_rows(arr: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """L2-normalize a 1D or 2D float32 numpy array."""
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(a))
        return a / norm if norm > 1e-12 else a
    norms = np.linalg.norm(a, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    norms = np.where(norms > 1e-12, norms, 1.0)
    return a / norms


def _normalize_text_for_tokens(text: str) -> str:
    """Lowercase and strip possessive "'s" while preserving contractions like "isn't"."""
    s = text.strip().lower().replace("\u2019", "'").replace("\u2018", "'").replace("`", "'")
    return re.sub(r"(?<!n)'s\b", "", s)


def _compute_file_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    """Return hex SHA-256 digest of a file on disk."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        while True:
            chunk = f.read(65536)
            if not chunk:
                break
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest().lower()


_SHA256_CACHE: dict[tuple[str, int, int], str] = {}
_SHA256_CACHE_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def _compute_file_sha256_cached(path: Path) -> str:
    try:
        st = path.stat()
    except OSError:
        return ""
    key = (str(path.resolve()), int(st.st_size), int(st.st_mtime_ns))
    with _SHA256_CACHE_LOCK:
        cached = _SHA256_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached
    actual = _compute_file_sha256(path)
    with _SHA256_CACHE_LOCK:
        if len(_SHA256_CACHE) >= 16:
            _SHA256_CACHE.clear()
        _SHA256_CACHE[key] = actual
    return actual


def _verify_sha256(
    path: Path,
    allowed_hashes: set[str],
    env_override_key: str,
    source_label: str = "",
) -> bool:
    if not path.exists():
        return False
    if os.environ.get("MESH_SKIP_CHECKSUM", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return True
    custom = os.environ.get(env_override_key, "").strip().lower()
    expected = {custom} if custom else {h.lower() for h in allowed_hashes}
    if not expected:
        return True
    try:
        actual = _compute_file_sha256_cached(path)
        if actual in expected:
            return True
        expected_list = sorted(expected)
        src_info = f" from {source_label}" if source_label else ""
        err_msg = (
            f"SHA-256 checksum mismatch for '{path.name}'{src_info}: "
            f"expected one of {expected_list}, got '{actual}'"
        )
        _emit_warning(err_msg)
        _record_model_status(
            state="checksum_mismatch",
            backend=_model_backend or "hash-fallback",
            last_error=err_msg,
            checksum_expected=expected_list,
            checksum_actual=actual,
        )
        return False
    except OSError as exc:
        err_msg = f"Failed reading '{path}' for SHA-256 verification: {exc}"
        _emit_warning(err_msg)
        _record_model_status(
            state="io_error",
            backend=_model_backend or "hash-fallback",
            last_error=err_msg,
        )
        return False


def _is_interactive_tty() -> bool:
    if os.environ.get("MESH_NON_INTERACTIVE", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return False
    return bool(
        hasattr(sys.stdin, "isatty")
        and sys.stdin.isatty()
        and hasattr(sys.stdout, "isatty")
        and sys.stdout.isatty()
    )


def _confirm_background_action(
    action_description: str,
    env_disable_key: Optional[str] = None,
    assume_yes: bool = False,
) -> bool:
    if env_disable_key and os.environ.get(env_disable_key, "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return False
    from .daemon import request_user_permission

    return request_user_permission(action_description, assume_yes=assume_yes)


def _request_model_download_permission(assume_yes: bool = False) -> bool:
    global _download_permission_decided, _download_permission_granted
    if os.environ.get("MESH_NO_MODEL_DOWNLOAD", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        if not _download_permission_decided:
            _download_permission_decided = True
            _download_permission_granted = False
            _record_model_status(
                state="download_disabled",
                backend="hash-fallback",
                last_error="ONNX download disabled via MESH_NO_MODEL_DOWNLOAD=1",
                hint="Unset MESH_NO_MODEL_DOWNLOAD and run mesh setup",
            )
        return False
    if assume_yes or os.environ.get("MESH_AUTO_APPROVE", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        _download_permission_decided = True
        _download_permission_granted = True
        return True
    if _download_permission_decided:
        return _download_permission_granted

    if not _is_interactive_tty():
        _download_permission_decided = True
        _download_permission_granted = False
        _record_model_status(
            state="download_skipped_non_interactive",
            backend="hash-fallback",
            last_error="ONNX model not downloaded and current process is non-interactive (no TTY).",
            hint="run mesh setup",
        )
        return False

    granted = _confirm_background_action(
        f"download the ~90MB ONNX embedding model into {_get_onnx_model_dir()}",
        env_disable_key="MESH_NO_MODEL_DOWNLOAD",
        assume_yes=assume_yes,
    )
    _download_permission_decided = True
    _download_permission_granted = granted
    if not granted:
        _record_model_status(
            state="download_declined",
            backend="hash-fallback",
            last_error="User declined ONNX model download prompt.",
            hint="run mesh setup",
        )
    return granted


def _split_long_text_windows(text: str, max_words: int = 180, stride: int = 140) -> list[str]:
    words = text.split()
    if len(words) <= max_words:
        return [text]
    windows: list[str] = []
    for start in range(0, len(words), stride):
        chunk = " ".join(words[start : start + max_words])
        if chunk:
            windows.append(chunk)
        if start + max_words >= len(words):
            break
        if len(windows) >= 8:
            break
    return windows or [text]


class _DirectOnnxEmbedding:
    """
    Ultra-fast, zero-network local ONNX Runtime + HuggingFace Tokenizers engine
    for 384-dim `all-MiniLM-L6-v2` embeddings (~80ms cold load, ~2ms inference).
    """

    def __init__(self, model_path: Path, tokenizer_path: Path):
        import onnxruntime as ort
        from tokenizers import Tokenizer

        self.model_path = model_path
        self.tokenizer_path = tokenizer_path
        self.tokenizer = Tokenizer.from_file(str(tokenizer_path))
        self.tokenizer.enable_truncation(max_length=256)
        self.tokenizer.enable_padding(pad_id=0, pad_token="[PAD]")

        sess_options = ort.SessionOptions()
        sess_options.graph_optimization_level = ort.GraphOptimizationLevel.ORT_ENABLE_ALL
        cpu_count = os.cpu_count() or 4
        sess_options.intra_op_num_threads = min(2, max(1, cpu_count // 2))
        sess_options.inter_op_num_threads = 1
        sess_options.execution_mode = ort.ExecutionMode.ORT_SEQUENTIAL
        try:
            sess_options.add_session_config_entry("session.intra_op.allow_spinning", "0")
        except Exception:
            pass
        sess_options.log_severity_level = 3

        self.session = ort.InferenceSession(
            str(model_path),
            sess_options=sess_options,
            providers=["CPUExecutionProvider"],
        )
        self.input_names = {inp.name for inp in self.session.get_inputs()}

    def _encode_raw_batches(self, texts: list[str], batch_size: int = 32) -> np.ndarray:
        if not texts:
            return np.empty((0, EMBEDDING_DIM), dtype=np.float32)
        all_batches: list[np.ndarray] = []
        for start in range(0, len(texts), batch_size):
            batch = texts[start : start + batch_size]
            encodings = self.tokenizer.encode_batch(batch)
            input_ids = np.asarray([e.ids for e in encodings], dtype=np.int64)
            attention_mask = np.asarray([e.attention_mask for e in encodings], dtype=np.int64)

            ort_inputs: dict[str, Any] = {}
            if "input_ids" in self.input_names:
                ort_inputs["input_ids"] = input_ids
            if "attention_mask" in self.input_names:
                ort_inputs["attention_mask"] = attention_mask
            if "token_type_ids" in self.input_names:
                ort_inputs["token_type_ids"] = np.zeros_like(input_ids, dtype=np.int64)

            outputs = self.session.run(None, ort_inputs)
            token_embeddings = np.asarray(outputs[0], dtype=np.float32)
            if token_embeddings.ndim == 3:
                mask = np.expand_dims(attention_mask, axis=-1).astype(np.float32)
                summed = np.sum(token_embeddings * mask, axis=1)
                counts = np.clip(mask.sum(axis=1), a_min=1e-9, a_max=None)
                pooled = summed / counts
            else:
                pooled = token_embeddings
            all_batches.append(_normalize_rows(pooled))
        if len(all_batches) == 1:
            return all_batches[0]
        return np.vstack(all_batches)

    def encode(self, texts: list[str], batch_size: int = 32) -> np.ndarray:
        if not texts:
            return np.empty((0, EMBEDDING_DIM), dtype=np.float32)
        if all(len(t) <= 900 for t in texts):
            return self._encode_raw_batches(texts, batch_size=batch_size)

        flat_windows: list[str] = []
        window_ranges: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
        for t in texts:
            wins = _split_long_text_windows(t)
            start_idx = len(flat_windows)
            flat_windows.extend(wins)
            window_ranges.append((start_idx, len(flat_windows)))

        win_vecs = self._encode_raw_batches(flat_windows, batch_size=batch_size)
        doc_vecs: list[np.ndarray] = []
        for start_idx, end_idx in window_ranges:
            if end_idx - start_idx == 1:
                doc_vecs.append(win_vecs[start_idx])
            else:
                mean_vec = np.mean(win_vecs[start_idx:end_idx], axis=0)
                doc_vecs.append(_normalize_rows(mean_vec))
        return np.vstack(doc_vecs)


def _stem_word(w: str) -> str:
    if len(w) <= 3 or "'" in w:
        return w
    if w.endswith("ies") and len(w) > 4:
        return w[:-3] + "y"
    if w.endswith("ing") and len(w) > 5:
        base = w[:-3]
        return base[:-1] if len(base) >= 3 and base[-1] == base[-2] else base
    if w.endswith("ed") and len(w) > 4:
        return w[:-2]
    if w.endswith("es") and len(w) > 4:
        return w[:-2]
    if w.endswith("s") and not w.endswith("ss") and len(w) > 3:
        return w[:-1]
    return w


def _hash_embed_384(text: str) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Concept-aware deterministic 384-dim semantic feature-hash embedding (<0.05ms).
    Reserves indices 368..383 == 0.0 as a deterministic watermark alongside the SQLite
    `embedding_model = 'hash-384'` column so warm-up vectors are always upgraded once ONNX loads.
    """
    if isinstance(text, str):
        _native_vec = _c_hash_embed_384(text)  # ~30 us vs ~330 us; None => Python path below
        if _native_vec is not None:
            return _native_vec
    vec = np.zeros(EMBEDDING_DIM, dtype=np.float32)
    cleaned = _normalize_text_for_tokens(text)
    raw_words = _WORD_TOKEN_RE.findall(cleaned)
    nums = _NUMBER_TOKEN_RE.findall(cleaned)

    content_words = [w for w in raw_words if w not in _STOPWORDS or w in _NEGATION_TOKENS]
    if not content_words and not nums:
        content_words = raw_words or ["empty"]

    stemmed_words = [_stem_word(w) for w in content_words]
    has_non_framing = any(sw not in {"user", "agent", "system"} for sw in stemmed_words)
    word_set = set(raw_words) | set(stemmed_words)

    features: list[tuple[str, float]] = []
    for w, sw in zip(content_words, stemmed_words):
        w_weight = 0.25 if (has_non_framing and sw in {"user", "agent", "system"}) else 1.35
        features.append((f"w:{sw}", w_weight))
        if w != sw:
            features.append((f"raw:{w}", w_weight * 0.45))

    for n in nums:
        features.append((f"num:{n}", 1.6))

    for i in range(len(stemmed_words) - 1):
        features.append((f"bi:{stemmed_words[i]}_{stemmed_words[i + 1]}", 0.65))

    for cid, cwords in _CONCEPT_CLUSTERS:
        overlap = len(word_set & cwords)
        if overlap > 0:
            features.append((cid, 1.5 + min(1.2, overlap * 0.4)))

    for sw in stemmed_words:
        if len(sw) >= 4:
            for j in range(len(sw) - 2):
                features.append((f"c3:{sw[j : j + 3]}", 0.22))

    for feat, weight in features:
        digest = hashlib.blake2b(feat.encode("utf-8"), digest_size=8).digest()
        h = int.from_bytes(digest, "little")
        idx1 = h % 368
        sign1 = 1.0 if ((h >> 9) & 1) == 0 else -1.0
        idx2 = (h >> 16) % 368
        sign2 = 1.0 if ((h >> 25) & 1) == 0 else -1.0
        idx3 = (h >> 32) % 368
        sign3 = 1.0 if ((h >> 41) & 1) == 0 else -1.0
        vec[idx1] += sign1 * weight
        vec[idx2] += sign2 * (weight * 0.6)
        vec[idx3] += sign3 * (weight * 0.3)

    return _normalize_rows(vec)


_LOCAL_PAIR_CACHE: Optional[tuple[Path, int, int, Path, int, int, Optional[tuple[Path, Path]]]] = None
_LOCAL_PAIR_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def _has_local_onnx_pair() -> Optional[tuple[Path, Path]]:
    global _LOCAL_PAIR_CACHE
    model_dir = _get_onnx_model_dir()
    target_model = model_dir / "model.onnx"
    target_tok = model_dir / "tokenizer.json"
    try:
        st_m = target_model.stat()
        st_t = target_tok.stat()
    except OSError:
        return None

    cache = _LOCAL_PAIR_CACHE
    if (
        cache is not None
        and cache[0] == target_model
        and cache[1] == st_m.st_mtime_ns
        and cache[2] == st_m.st_size
        and cache[3] == target_tok
        and cache[4] == st_t.st_mtime_ns
        and cache[5] == st_t.st_size
    ):
        return cache[6]

    res = None
    if st_m.st_size > 80_000_000 and st_t.st_size > 100_000:
        if _verify_sha256(
            target_model,
            _ALLOWED_MODEL_SHA256,
            "MESH_MODEL_SHA256",
            source_label=str(target_model),
        ) and _verify_sha256(
            target_tok,
            _ALLOWED_TOKENIZER_SHA256,
            "MESH_TOKENIZER_SHA256",
            source_label=str(target_tok),
        ):
            res = (target_model, target_tok)

    with _LOCAL_PAIR_LOCK:
        _LOCAL_PAIR_CACHE = (
            target_model,
            int(st_m.st_mtime_ns),
            int(st_m.st_size),
            target_tok,
            int(st_t.st_mtime_ns),
            int(st_t.st_size),
            res,
        )
    return res


_DISCOVER_NEG_UNTIL: float = 0.0


def _discover_negative_ttl() -> float:
    raw = os.environ.get("MESH_MODEL_DISCOVERY_TTL", "").strip()
    if not raw:
        return 120.0
    try:
        return max(0.0, float(raw))
    except ValueError:
        return 120.0


def _discover_local_onnx_pair() -> Optional[tuple[Path, Path]]:
    global _DISCOVER_NEG_UNTIL
    fast_pair = _has_local_onnx_pair()
    if fast_pair is not None:
        return fast_pair
    # A failed search walks several cache trees with rglob (thousands of is_file() calls,
    # ~12 ms on Windows) and _get_model() used to repeat it on EVERY learn()/recall().
    # Remember the miss; a pair that appears later is still picked up by the
    # _has_local_onnx_pair() fast check above.
    if time.monotonic() < _DISCOVER_NEG_UNTIL:
        return None

    model_dir = _get_onnx_model_dir()
    target_model = model_dir / "model.onnx"
    target_tok = model_dir / "tokenizer.json"

    home = Path.home()
    hf_hub = Path(os.environ.get("HF_HOME", str(home / ".cache" / "huggingface"))) / "hub"
    search_roots = [
        hf_hub / "models--sentence-transformers--all-MiniLM-L6-v2",
        hf_hub / "models--qdrant--all-MiniLM-L6-v2-onnx",
        Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "fastembed_cache",
        home / ".cache" / "fastembed",
    ]

    found_model: Optional[Path] = None
    found_tok: Optional[Path] = None
    for root in search_roots:
        if not root.exists():
            continue
        try:
            for candidate in root.rglob("*"):
                if not candidate.is_file():
                    continue
                name_lower = candidate.name.lower()
                if name_lower == "tokenizer.json" and found_tok is None:
                    if candidate.stat().st_size > 100_000 and _verify_sha256(
                        candidate,
                        _ALLOWED_TOKENIZER_SHA256,
                        "MESH_TOKENIZER_SHA256",
                        source_label=str(candidate),
                    ):
                        found_tok = candidate
                elif name_lower in ("model.onnx", "model_optimized.onnx") and found_model is None:
                    if candidate.stat().st_size > 80_000_000 and _verify_sha256(
                        candidate,
                        _ALLOWED_MODEL_SHA256,
                        "MESH_MODEL_SHA256",
                        source_label=str(candidate),
                    ):
                        found_model = candidate
                if found_model is not None and found_tok is not None:
                    break
        except OSError:
            continue

    target_tok_ok = target_tok.exists() and _verify_sha256(
        target_tok, _ALLOWED_TOKENIZER_SHA256, "MESH_TOKENIZER_SHA256", source_label=str(target_tok)
    )
    if found_tok is not None and not target_tok_ok:
        try:
            ensure_secure_mesh_dir(model_dir)
            shutil.copy2(found_tok, target_tok)
        except OSError:
            pass
    target_model_ok = target_model.exists() and _verify_sha256(
        target_model, _ALLOWED_MODEL_SHA256, "MESH_MODEL_SHA256", source_label=str(target_model)
    )
    if found_model is not None and not target_model_ok:
        try:
            ensure_secure_mesh_dir(model_dir)
            shutil.copy2(found_model, target_model)
        except OSError:
            pass

    _pair = _has_local_onnx_pair()
    if _pair is None:
        _ttl = _discover_negative_ttl()
        if _ttl > 0.0:
            _DISCOVER_NEG_UNTIL = time.monotonic() + _ttl
    return _pair


def _download_file_from_mirrors(
    urls: list[str],
    dest: Path,
    min_bytes: int,
    allowed_sha256: Optional[set[str]] = None,
    sha256_env_key: str = "",
) -> bool:
    if dest.exists():
        try:
            if dest.stat().st_size >= min_bytes:
                if not allowed_sha256 or _verify_sha256(
                    dest, allowed_sha256, sha256_env_key, source_label=str(dest)
                ):
                    return True
        except OSError:
            pass

    ensure_secure_mesh_dir(_get_onnx_model_dir())
    headers = {"User-Agent": f"mesh-context-layer/{VERSION}"}
    hf_token = (
        os.environ.get("HF_TOKEN")
        or os.environ.get("HUGGING_FACE_HUB_TOKEN")
        or os.environ.get("HUGGINGFACE_TOKEN")
    )
    try:
        import httpx

        timeout_cfg = httpx.Timeout(connect=3.5, read=15.0, write=5.0, pool=5.0)
        for url in urls:
            req_headers = dict(headers)
            if hf_token and "huggingface.co" in url:
                req_headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {hf_token.strip()}"
            tmp_path = dest.with_suffix(f".tmp.{os.getpid()}.{threading.get_ident()}")
            try:
                with httpx.Client(follow_redirects=True, timeout=timeout_cfg, headers=req_headers) as client:
                    with client.stream("GET", url) as resp:
                        if resp.status_code != 200:
                            err_msg = f"Download from {url} failed with HTTP status {resp.status_code}"
                            _emit_warning(err_msg)
                            _record_model_status(
                                state="download_http_error",
                                backend=_model_backend or "hash-fallback",
                                last_error=err_msg,
                            )
                            continue
                        written = 0
                        with open(tmp_path, "wb") as f:
                            for chunk in resp.iter_bytes(chunk_size=65536):
                                if chunk:
                                    written += len(chunk)
                                    if written > _MAX_TAR_EXTRACT_BYTES:
                                        raise ValueError("Download exceeded maximum allowed size")
                                    f.write(chunk)
                if tmp_path.exists() and tmp_path.stat().st_size >= min_bytes:
                    if allowed_sha256 and not _verify_sha256(
                        tmp_path, allowed_sha256, sha256_env_key, source_label=url
                    ):
                        tmp_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                        continue
                    tmp_path.replace(dest)
                    return True
                else:
                    actual_sz = tmp_path.stat().st_size if tmp_path.exists() else 0
                    err_msg = f"Downloaded file from {url} was truncated ({actual_sz} bytes < {min_bytes} bytes)"
                    _emit_warning(err_msg)
                    _record_model_status(
                        state="download_truncated",
                        backend=_model_backend or "hash-fallback",
                        last_error=err_msg,
                    )
            except Exception as exc:
                err_msg = f"Download error from {url}: {exc}"
                _emit_warning(err_msg)
                _record_model_status(
                    state="download_error",
                    backend=_model_backend or "hash-fallback",
                    last_error=err_msg,
                )
            finally:
                try:
                    tmp_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                except OSError:
                    pass
    except Exception as outer_exc:
        err_msg = f"HTTP client initialization failed during model download: {outer_exc}"
        _emit_warning(err_msg)
        _record_model_status(
            state="download_error",
            backend=_model_backend or "hash-fallback",
            last_error=err_msg,
        )
    return False


def _safe_copy_limited(src: Any, dst: Any, max_bytes: int = _MAX_TAR_EXTRACT_BYTES) -> bool:
    total = 0
    while True:
        buf = src.read(65536)
        if not buf:
            return True
        total += len(buf)
        if total > max_bytes:
            return False
        dst.write(buf)


def _download_from_gcs_tarball() -> bool:
    if not _GCS_TARBALL_MIRRORS:
        return False
    if _has_local_onnx_pair() is not None:
        return True
    model_dir = _get_onnx_model_dir()
    target_model = model_dir / "model.onnx"
    target_tok = model_dir / "tokenizer.json"
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir(model_dir)
    tmp_tar = model_dir / f"bundle.{os.getpid()}.{threading.get_ident()}.tar.gz"
    try:
        if not _download_file_from_mirrors(_GCS_TARBALL_MIRRORS, tmp_tar, min_bytes=20_000_000):
            return False
        with tarfile.open(tmp_tar, "r:gz") as tar:
            for member in tar.getmembers():
                if not member.isfile() or member.size <= 0 or member.size > _MAX_TAR_EXTRACT_BYTES:
                    continue
                base_name = Path(member.name).name.lower()
                if base_name in ("model.onnx", "model_optimized.onnx") and not target_model.exists():
                    extracted = tar.extractfile(member)
                    if extracted is not None:
                        tmp_out = target_model.with_suffix(f".tmp.{os.getpid()}")
                        with open(tmp_out, "wb") as out_f:
                            ok = _safe_copy_limited(extracted, out_f, _MAX_TAR_EXTRACT_BYTES)
                        source_lbl = f"{_GCS_TARBALL_MIRRORS[0]}:{member.name}" if _GCS_TARBALL_MIRRORS else member.name
                        if ok and _verify_sha256(
                            tmp_out,
                            _ALLOWED_MODEL_SHA256,
                            "MESH_MODEL_SHA256",
                            source_label=source_lbl,
                        ):
                            tmp_out.replace(target_model)
                        else:
                            tmp_out.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                elif base_name == "tokenizer.json" and not target_tok.exists():
                    extracted = tar.extractfile(member)
                    if extracted is not None:
                        tmp_out = target_tok.with_suffix(f".tmp.{os.getpid()}")
                        with open(tmp_out, "wb") as out_f:
                            ok = _safe_copy_limited(extracted, out_f, 20 * 1024 * 1024)
